# Imperator formable decisions (Invictus / Terra Indomita) as EU5 formables, 2026-11-07.
# Tiers -> formable level: tier 1 = 2, tier 2 = 3, endgame = 4. rule: historical, plausible for endgame and late-antique formables.
# Geography: Imperator provinces -> EU5 locations (province table); Imperator areas/regions -> the EU5 provinces holding most of
# their provinces (geometry triggers of the event import where they exist). required_locations_fraction 1 for province lists,
# 0.8 for areas/regions, or the Imperator percentage/count. Imperator name, adjective, colour and tag changes are the formable fields.
# Formables only reachable through tags, dates or missions, and the four Invictus disabled with always = no, are not imported.
#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: highlight (map highlight in the decision) has no formable field; dropped everywhere

############ misc ############

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/colchis_decisions.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was area abasgoi_area (province table, majority per EU5 province) in IR
form_abasgia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator misc
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:abasgoi # Imperator primary_culture = abasgoi
		#P:A Removed: tag = ABG (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		political_influence >= 50
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that ABG does not exist yet (formable_not_abasgia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = ABASGIA_NAME
	flag = ABASGIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = ABASGIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = ABG
	color = abasgia_color

	locations = {
		anacopia
		anaklia
		batumi
		bedia
		lata_georgia
		ozurgeti
		pitsunda
		poti_caucasus
		sukhumi
		tsalenjikha
	}

	form_effect = {
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_small_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		add_political_influence = 25 # influence_large
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_aeolia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Elaea (279), Adramyttion (281), Atarna (282), Mytilene (283), Eresos (284), Kyme (285), Temnos (296), Aigai (298), Kyme (285) | Imperator required 6 of 9 provinces in IR
form_aeolia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:greek_group } # Imperator hellenic
		capital ?= {
			is_coastal = yes
		}
		OR = {
			culture = culture:aeolian # Imperator primary_culture = aeolian
			capital ?= {
				OR = {
					this = location:bergama # Imperator Elaea (279)
					this = location:edremit # Imperator Adramyttion (281)
					this = location:edremit # Imperator Atarna (282)
					this = location:mitilene # Imperator Mytilene (283)
					this = location:molyvos # Imperator Eresos (284)
					this = location:foca # Imperator Kyme (285)
					this = location:menemen # Imperator Temnos (296)
					this = location:foca # Imperator Aigai (298)
					this = location:foca # Imperator Kyme (285)
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that IOA does not exist yet (formable_not_aeolia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
	}

	name = IOA
	flag = IOA #P:A mod flag
	adjective = IOA_ADJ
	tag = IOA
	color = aeolia_color

	locations = {
		bergama
		edremit
		foca
		menemen
		mitilene
		molyvos
	}

	form_effect = {
		# custom_tooltip = form_aeolia_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
		capital ?= {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
			# area:ionia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area ionia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:mysia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area mysia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_aestuia.txt
form_aestuia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical
	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:aestian # Imperator primary_culture = aestian
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		#P:A Removed: tag = AUA (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 10 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 10 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that AUA does not exist yet (formable_not_aestuia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = any_aestian_country_tt
			OR = {
				any_country = {
					count = all
					# filter = {
					# 	primary_culture = aestian
					# 	num_of_cities >= 1
					# 	NOT = { this = root }
					# }
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator filter was not translated
					is_subject_of = root
				}
				AND = {
					num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
					any_country = {
						count = 1
						culture = culture:aestian # Imperator primary_culture = aestian
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = AESTUIA_NAME
	flag = AUA #P:A flag from the coat of arms import on dev
	adjective = AESTUIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = AUA
	color = map_AUA


	form_effect = {
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = aestuia_modifier
			days = 7300
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_subject = {
					culture = culture:aestian # Imperator primary_culture = aestian
				}
			}
			# every_subject = {
			# 	limit = { primary_culture = aestian }
			# 	every_owned_province = {
			# 		limit = {
			# 			OR = { is_in_region = germania_region is_in_region = vistulia_region is_in_region = bohemia_area }
			# 		}
			# 		set_owned_by = root
			# 	}
			# }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: nothing inside Imperator every_subject could be translated
		}
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_alamannia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Vindonissa (3633), Vitudurum (3634), Constantia (3635), Vernania (3637), Arbor Felix (3639), Tasgetium (3732), Vemania (3739), Raetia Occidentalis (3745), Raetia Orientalis (3746), Brigantium (3636), Summuntorium (3775), Rapis (3750), Caelius Mons (3741), Cassiliacum (3740), Cambodunum (3638), Navoa (3747), Summuntorium (3775), Losodica (3769), Ad Fines Germaniae (3772), Hermunduria (3804), Sedusia (3800) | area raurica_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area tribocia_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area vangionia_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area latobrigia_area (province table, majority per EU5 province) in IR
form_alamannia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		has_global_variable = ahistorical_formables_variable
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:irminonic # Imperator primary_culture = irminonic
		#P:A Removed: tag = ALM (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 6 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 6 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_region_germania_superior_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_central_gaul_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_rhaetia_region = yes
			}
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that ALM does not exist yet (alamannia_not_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = formable_alamannia_capital
			capital ?= {
				OR = {
					pa_imp_in_region_germania_superior_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_central_gaul_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_rhaetia_region = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = ALAMANNIA_NAME
	flag = ALAMANNIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = ALAMANNIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = ALM
	color = alamannia_color

	locations = {
		backnang
		baden
		baden_im_aargau
		balingen
		belfort
		benfeld
		biberach
		bregenz
		bruchsal
		colmar
		darmstadt
		durlach
		ellwangen
		ensisheim
		erbach
		erbach_swabia
		ferrette
		frauenfeld
		freiburg
		furstenberg
		goppingen
		hachberg
		haguenau
		heidenheim
		heilbronn
		helfenstein
		immenstadt
		kempten
		krumbach
		kyburg
		lichtenberg
		memmingen
		mosbach
		mulhouse
		mullheim
		murbach
		nagold
		oberdorf
		oberkirch
		oberndorf
		oettingen
		offenbach
		ohringen
		ravensburg
		sigmaringen
		sinsheim
		st_gallen
		stockach
		strasbourg
		stuttgart
		tubingen
		ulm
		wertingen
		zabern
	}

	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = tribe REFORM = tribal_federation }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:sarayjuk # Imperator Orlik (8939)

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_alba.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was area caledonia_meridionalis_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area caledonia_occidentalis_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area caledonia_septentrionalis_area (province table, majority per EU5 province) in IR
form_alba = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		has_global_variable = ahistorical_formables_variable
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = culture:ivernian # Imperator primary_culture = ivernian
			culture = culture:voluntian # Imperator primary_culture = voluntian
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = SCO (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 6 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 6 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_meridionalis_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_occidentalis_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_septentrionalis_area = yes
			}
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that SCO does not exist yet (formable_not_alba_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = formable_alba_capital
			capital ?= {
				OR = {
					pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_meridionalis_area = yes
					pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_occidentalis_area = yes
					pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_septentrionalis_area = yes
				}
			}
		}
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = ALBA_NAME
	flag = ALBA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = ALBA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = SCO
	color = alba_color

	locations = {
		aberdeen
		ardtornish
		ayr
		banff
		ben_klibreck
		ben_nevis
		berwick
		buchan
		carn_eige
		cowal
		dingwall
		dornoch
		dumfries
		dunbar
		dundee
		dunfermline
		duns
		durness
		fannichs
		forfar
		gairloch
		inverary
		inverurie
		irvine
		kenmure
		kintyre
		kirkcudbright
		lewis
		linlithgow
		lochaber
		mar
		oban
		roxburgh
		selkirk
		skye
		st_andrews
		stirling
		stonehaven
		stranraer
		thurso
		tongue
		uist
		ullapool
	}

	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = tribe REFORM = tribal_federation }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = inherit_alba_provinces_tooltip
		hidden_effect = {
			pa_imp_formable_conquest_effect = { TRIGGER = pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_meridionalis_area } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator handed every AI-owned province of the area caledonia_meridionalis_area to the new country; EU5 uses the geography trigger
			pa_imp_formable_conquest_effect = { TRIGGER = pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_occidentalis_area } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator handed every AI-owned province of the area caledonia_occidentalis_area to the new country; EU5 uses the geography trigger
			pa_imp_formable_conquest_effect = { TRIGGER = pa_imp_in_area_caledonia_septentrionalis_area } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator handed every AI-owned province of the area caledonia_septentrionalis_area to the new country; EU5 uses the geography trigger
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					root = {
						government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
					}
				}
				pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
				pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
				pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_bastetania.txt

form_bastetania = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:bastetanian # Imperator primary_culture = bastetanian
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		#P:A Removed: tag = BTT (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 7 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 7 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that BTT does not exist yet (formable_not_bastetania_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = BASTETANIA_NAME
	flag = BASTETANIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = BASTETANIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = BTT
	color = bastetania_purple

		illora

		lorca

		mula
		murcia
		nevada_range
		orgiva
		orihuela
		pinar
		segura_de_la_sierra
		segura_range
		subbetic_range
		velez_malaga
		velez_rubio
	}

	form_effect = {

			}
			#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Menova (1366) in IR

			pa_imp_formable_conquest_effect = { TRIGGER = pa_imp_in_area_mastiania_area } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator handed every AI-owned province of the area mastiania_area to the new country; EU5 uses the geography trigger

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_boeotia.txt

form_boeotia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:boeotian # Imperator primary_culture = boeotian
		NOR = {
			tag = BOE
			tag = THE
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_in_area_boeotia_area = yes
		}
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations <= 12 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities <= 12 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that BOT does not exist yet (boeotia_not_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = BOEOTIA_NAME
	flag = BOT #P:A TODO No flag: Imperator has no coat of arms BOT; the mod's random Imperator-style flag rules apply
	adjective = BOEOTIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = BOT
	color = boeotian_brown

	locations = {
		carrickmacross
		dunluce
		livadeia
		thebes
	}

	form_effect = {
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_small_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		add_political_influence = 25 # influence_large
		pa_imp_add_1_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_brittany.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Conbaristum (3308), Namnetum (2212), Foricum (2215), Sipia (2144) | area armorica_minoris_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area armorica_centralis_area (province table, majority per EU5 province) in IR
form_brittany = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		has_global_variable = ahistorical_formables_variable
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:britannic_group } # Imperator britannic
		#P:A Removed: tag = BTN (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		NOT = {
			culture = culture:ivernian # Imperator primary_culture = ivernian
			culture = culture:voluntian # Imperator primary_culture = voluntian
			culture = culture:caledonian # Imperator primary_culture = caledonian
			culture = culture:taexalian # Imperator primary_culture = taexalian
			culture = culture:votadinian # Imperator primary_culture = votadinian
			culture = culture:damnonian # Imperator primary_culture = damnonian
		}
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 6 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 6 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_region_armorica_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_aquitaine_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_transalpine_gaul_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_belgica_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_germania_superior_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_central_gaul_region = yes
			}
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that BTN does not exist yet (brittany_not_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = formable_brittany_capital
			capital ?= {
				OR = {
					pa_imp_in_region_armorica_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_aquitaine_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_transalpine_gaul_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_belgica_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_germania_superior_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_region_central_gaul_region = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = BRITTANY_NAME
	flag = BRITTANY_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = BRITTANY_ADJECTIVE
	tag = BTN
	color = brittany_color

	locations = {
		blain
		brest
		carhaix
		chateaubriant
		chatelaudren
		dol
		fougeres
		guerande
		hennebont
		josselin
		le_faou
		machecoul
		monfort_sur_meu
		nantes
		ploermel
		quimper
		quimperle
		redon
		rennes
		rohan
		saint_brieuc
		saint_malo
		st_pol_de_leon
		treguier
		vannes
		vitre
	}

	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = tribe REFORM = tribal_federation }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

	}

								this = location:limassol # Imperator Kourion (7990)
								this = location:marion # Imperator Marion (6431)
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {

							OR = {
								this = location:marion # Imperator Marion (6431)
							}
						}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_gallaecia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was area callaecia_septentrionalis_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area callaecia_meridionalis_area (province table, majority per EU5 province) in IR
form_gallaecia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:callaecian # Imperator primary_culture = callaecian
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		#P:A Removed: tag = GLL (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 7 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 7 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that GLL does not exist yet (formable_not_gallaecia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = GALLAECIA_NAME
	flag = GALLAECIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = GALLAECIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = GLL
	color = gallaecia_color

	locations = {
		barcelos
		barco_de_valdeorras
		braga
		finisterre
		ginzo_de_limia
		guimaraes
		lalin
		lugo
		mellid
		monforte_de_lemos
		monterrey
		navia_de_suarna
		noya
		ourense
		pontevedra
		porto
		ribadavia
		santiago_compostela
		sarria
		tuy
		valenca
		viana_bolo
		viana_do_castelo
		villalba
	}

	form_effect = {
		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		custom_tooltip = inherit_gallaecia_provinces_tooltip
		hidden_effect = {
			pa_imp_formable_conquest_effect = { TRIGGER = pa_imp_in_area_callaecia_septentrionalis_area } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator handed every AI-owned province of the area callaecia_septentrionalis_area to the new country; EU5 uses the geography trigger
			pa_imp_formable_conquest_effect = { TRIGGER = pa_imp_in_area_callaecia_meridionalis_area } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator handed every AI-owned province of the area callaecia_meridionalis_area to the new country; EU5 uses the geography trigger
		}
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

form_liguria = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1

	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:ligurian # Imperator primary_culture = ligurian
		#P:A Removed: tag = LIG (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 6 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 6 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that LIG does not exist yet (formable_not_liguria_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = LIGURIA_NAME
	flag = LIGURIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = LIGURIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = LIG
	color = liguria_color

	locations = {
		acqui
		albenga
		asti
		cairo_montenotte
		casale
		draguignan
		finalborgo
		genoa
		grasse
		lavagna
		mondovi
		sarzana
		savona
		ventimiglia
	}

	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = tribe REFORM = tribal_federation }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_1_formables/form_lydia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Sardis (292), Temnos (296), Myloukome (293), Thyateira (274), Larisa Ionias (294) in IR
form_lydia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = culture:lydian # Imperator primary_culture = lydian
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = LYD (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					culture = culture:lydian # Imperator primary_culture = lydian
				}
			}
			culture = culture:lydian # Imperator primary_culture = lydian
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = formable_not_lydia_exists
			NOR = {
				any_country = {
					tag = SRD
					num_locations >= 1 # Imperator has_land
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				any_country = {
					#P:A Removed: tag = LYD (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
					num_locations >= 1 # Imperator has_land
				}
			}
		}
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = LYD
	flag = SARDIS_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = LYD_ADJ
	tag = LYD
	color = lighter_dark_orange

	locations = {
		akhisar
		alasehir
		manisa
		menemen
		tire
	}

	form_effect = {
		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		# custom_tooltip = form_lydia_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
			# area:lydia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area lydia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_small_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

form_rhaetia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1

		culture = culture:rhaetian # Imperator primary_culture = rhaetian
		#P:A Removed: tag = RAE (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 10 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 10 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that RAE does not exist yet (formable_not_rhaetia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify

			text = any_rhaetian_country_tt
			OR = {
				any_country = {
					count = all
					# filter = {
					# 	primary_culture = rhaetian

					# 	NOT = { this = root }
					# }
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator filter was not translated
					is_subject_of = root
				}
				AND = {
					num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
					any_country = {
						count = 1
						culture = culture:rhaetian # Imperator primary_culture = rhaetian
					}
				}
			}
		}
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = RHAETIA_NAME
	flag = RAE #P:A mod flag
	adjective = RHAETIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = RAE
	# color #P:A TODO Imperator colour rhaetia_color is defined nowhere (Invictus, Indomita or Imperator); the country keeps its old map colour

		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		if = {

				}

		}

	}

form_suebia = {
	level = 2 # Imperator tier 1
	required_locations_fraction = 1

	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

			culture = culture:suebian # Imperator primary_culture = suebian
		}
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		#P:A Removed: tag = SBN (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 10 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 10 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_1_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that SBN does not exist yet (formable_not_suebia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = any_suebian_country_tt
				OR = {
					any_country = {
						count = all
						# filter = {
						# 	OR = { primary_culture = irminonic primary_culture = suebian }
						# 	num_of_cities >= 1
						# 	NOT = { this = root }
						# }
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator filter was not translated
						is_subject_of = root
					}
					AND = {
						num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
						any_country = {
							count = 1
							OR = {
								culture = culture:irminonic # Imperator primary_culture = irminonic
								culture = culture:suebian # Imperator primary_culture = suebian
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				pa_imp_owns_ir_area_semnonia_area = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_area = semnonia_area; EU5 provinces standing in for it
				pa_imp_owns_ir_area_pharodenia_area = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_area = pharodenia_area; EU5 provinces standing in for it
				pa_imp_owns_ir_area_langobardia_area = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_area = langobardia_area; EU5 provinces standing in for it
				pa_imp_owns_ir_area_suradonia_area = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_area = suradonia_area; EU5 provinces standing in for it
				pa_imp_owns_ir_area_caluconia_area = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_area = caluconia_area; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			}
		}
	}

	name = SUEBIA_NAME
	flag = SBN #P:A TODO No flag: Imperator has no coat of arms SBN; the mod's random Imperator-style flag rules apply
	adjective = SUEBIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = SBN
	color = suebia_color


	form_effect = {
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = suebia_modifier
			days = 7300
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_subject = {
					OR = {
						culture = culture:irminonic # Imperator primary_culture = irminonic
						culture = culture:suebian # Imperator primary_culture = suebian
					}
				}
			}
			# every_subject = {
			# 	limit = {
			# 		OR = { primary_culture = irminonic primary_culture = suebian }
			# 	}
			# 	every_owned_province = {

			# 		}
			# 		set_owned_by = root
			# 	}
			# }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: nothing inside Imperator every_subject could be translated

		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_achaean_league.txt
form_achaean_league = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical
	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:greek_group } # Imperator hellenic
		government_type = government_type:republic # Imperator is_republic = yes
		OR = {
			culture = culture:achaean # Imperator primary_culture = achaean
			culture = culture:arcadian # Imperator primary_culture = arcadian
			capital ?= {
				OR = {
					pa_imp_in_area_achaea_area = yes
					pa_imp_in_area_arcadia_area = yes
				}
			}
		}
		government_type = government_type:republic # Imperator is_republic = yes
		# tag = ARK
		#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator tag ARK has no matching mod country
		#P:A Removed: tag = ACL (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		# tag = HLA
		#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator tag HLA has no matching mod country
		# tag = ACH
		#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator tag ACH has no matching mod country
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that ACL does not exist yet ("formable_not_achaean_league_exists"); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = achaean_league_cooldown
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = achaean_league_cooldown_tt
				NOT = {
					has_variable = achaean_league_cooldown
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:vostitsa # Imperator Aigion (444)
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:patras # Imperator Patrai (434)
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:chalandritsa # Imperator Dyme (7890)
		}
		trigger_else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				culture = culture:achaean # Imperator primary_culture = achaean
			}
			political_influence >= 150
			OR = {
				AND = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = sicyon_not_held_by_diadochi
						# c:PRY = { owns_or_subject_owns = 8003 }
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator c:PRY was not translated
						# c:EGY = { owns_or_subject_owns = 8003 }
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator c:EGY was not translated
						# c:MAC = { owns_or_subject_owns = 8003 }
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator c:MAC was not translated
						# c:TRE = { owns_or_subject_owns = 8003 }
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator c:TRE was not translated
						# c:SEL = { owns_or_subject_owns = 8003 }
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator c:SEL was not translated
					}
					num_locations >= 3 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 3 (territories); EU5 owned locations
				}
				AND = {
					owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:vostitsa # Imperator Aigion (444)
					owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:patras # Imperator Patrai (434)
					owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:chalandritsa # Imperator Dyme (7890)
					owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:xylokastro # Imperator Pellene (7893)
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			political_influence >= 150
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:vostitsa # Imperator Aigion (444)
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:patras # Imperator Patrai (434)
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:chalandritsa # Imperator Dyme (7890)
			owns_or_non_sovereign_subject_owns = location:xylokastro # Imperator Pellene (7893)
		}
	}

	name = ACHAEA_NAME
	flag = ACHAEAN_LEAGUE_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = ACHAEA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = ACL
	# color #P:A TODO Imperator colour achaean_league_color is defined nowhere (Invictus, Indomita or Imperator); the country keeps its old map colour


	form_effect = {
		capital ?= {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			add_political_influence = -150
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject = no
			}
			# trigger_event = { id = dhe_achaean_league.1 }
			#P:A TODO Associated event was not imported: dhe_achaean_league.1
		}
		# custom_tooltip = form_achaean_league_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
			# region:greece_region = { formable_region_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator region greece_region not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
	}
}

			any_country = {
				num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
				#P:A Removed: tag = AIN (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
			}
		}
	}

	allow = {
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = AIN_NAME
	flag = AIN #P:A TODO No flag: Imperator has no coat of arms AIN; the mod's random Imperator-style flag rules apply
	adjective = AIN_ADJECTIVE
	tag = AIN
	color = navy_blue

	locations = {
		atsuta
		ishikari
		nakakamikawa
		sapporo
		sorachi
		uryuu
		yuubari
	}

	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = monarchy REFORM = theocratic_monarchy }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		random_owned_location = {
			limit = {
				# this = capital_scope
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator this was not translated
			}
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
		}
		pa_imp_add_2_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_armenia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Armaouira (1570), Artemita Vaspurakan (998), Arxata (1542), Atarbegian (1589), Satala (1770), Tigranakert (1640), Karana (1744) in IR
form_armenia = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = {
			OR = {
				has_culture_group = culture_group:lydian_group #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator anatolian group -> the mod's lydian, lycian and luwian groups
				has_culture_group = culture_group:lycian_group
				has_culture_group = culture_group:luwian_group
			}
		}
		OR = {
			culture = culture:armenian_culture # Imperator primary_culture = armenian
			capital ?= {
				OR = {
					pa_imp_in_region_armenia_region = yes
					pa_imp_in_area_armenia_maior_area = yes
					pa_imp_in_area_armenia_minor_area = yes
					pa_imp_in_area_sophene_area = yes
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 12 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 12 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = ARM (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that ARM does not exist yet (armenia_must_not_exist); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = ARM_NAME
	flag = ARM #P:A mod flag
	adjective = ARM_ADJECTIVE
	tag = ARM
	color = map_ARM

	locations = {
		bjni
		catak
		erzurum
		gandzasar
		kelkit
		nakhchivan
		surmali
	}

	form_effect = {
		add_political_influence = 150 # Imperator monarchy_formable_government_change_effect
		if = {
			limit = {
				# p:1570 = { is_capital = no }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator p:1570 was not translated
			}
			set_capital = location:surmali #P:A TODO Verify placement, was Armaouira (1570) in IR
				# p:1570.state = { set_state_capital = 1570 }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator province 1570.state has no EU5 location
			capital ?= {
				if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
					limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
					change_location_rank = location_rank:town
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Armaouira (1570) in IR
		location:surmali = {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		# custom_tooltip = armenia_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
			# region:armenia_region = { formable_region_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator region armenia_region not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:armenia_maior_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area armenia_maior_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:armenia_minor_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area armenia_minor_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:sophene_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area sophene_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:corduene_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area corduene_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 30 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 30 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}

		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)

		multan
		pindi_gheb
		prayag
		sialkot
		uch
		varanasi
	}

			limit = {
				tag = YDD
			}
		}
		else_if = {

					tag = PRA
				}
			}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_babylon.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Babylon (918), Ctesiphon (912), Samarra (893), Borsippa (919), Apamea ad Tigridem (933), Larsa (939), Alexandria Susiana (940), Teredon (964), Daskara (897), Agines (4779), Shushan (946) in IR
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Agines (4779) in IR: mapped to location:harunabad, in a different EU5 region from the rest of the list; check it first
form_babylon = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = culture:babylonian # Imperator primary_culture = babylonian
			religion = religion:mesopotamian_pantheon # Imperator mesopotamian_religion
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = BBY (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 25 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 25 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that BBY does not exist yet (formable_not_babylon_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = BABYLON_NAME
	flag = BABYLON_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = BABYLON_ADJECTIVE
	tag = BBY
	color = babylon_color

	locations = {
		babylon
		borsippa
		datebir
		dur_yakin
		harunabad
		ktesiphon
		labanda
		larsa
		meymeh
		tikrit
	}

	form_effect = {
		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					capital ?= {
						this = location:babylon # Imperator Babylon (918)
					}
				}
			}
			set_capital = location:babylon #P:A TODO Verify placement, was Babylon (918) in IR
				# p:918.state = { set_state_capital = 918 }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator province 918.state has no EU5 location
			capital ?= {
				if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
					limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
					change_location_rank = location_rank:town
				}
			}
		}
		change_religion = religion:mesopotamian_pantheon # Imperator set_country_religion = mesopotamian_religion
		#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Babylon (918) in IR
		location:babylon = {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_large_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_large_effect = yes
		}
		# custom_tooltip = form_babylon_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
			# region:assyria_region = { formable_region_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator region assyria_region not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# region:mesopotamia_region = { formable_region_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator region mesopotamia_region not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:susiana_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area susiana_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:pars_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area pars_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = formable_country_huge_modifier
			days = 7600
		}
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = formed_babylon_inv
		}
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_caria.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Diospolis Lykou (1948), Tralles (1966), Alabanda (1967), Mylasa (1977), Halikarnassos (1981) in IR
form_caria = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = culture:carian # Imperator primary_culture = carian
			AND = {
				culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:greek_group } # Imperator hellenic
				OR = {
					capital ?= {
						pa_imp_in_area_coastal_caria_area = yes
					}
					capital ?= {
						pa_imp_in_area_inland_caria_area = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = CIA (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that CIA does not exist yet (formable_not_caria_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				NOT = {
					culture = culture:carian # Imperator primary_culture = carian
				}
				#P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator any_country_culture -> EU5 primary and accepted cultures
				any_primary_or_accepted_culture = {
					this = culture:carian # Imperator is_culture = carian
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = caria_citizen_nobles_tooltip
				#P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator any_integrated_culture -> EU5 primary and accepted cultures
				any_primary_or_accepted_culture = {
					this = culture:carian # Imperator is_culture = carian
				}
			}
		}
	}

	name = CIA
	flag = CIA #P:A flag from the coat of arms import on dev
	adjective = CIA_ADJ
	tag = CIA
	color = map_CIA

	locations = {
		bodrum
		cine
		guzelhisar
		lazkiye
		milas
	}

	form_effect = {
		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		# custom_tooltip = form_chrysaoric_league_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
		capital ?= {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
			# area:ionia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area ionia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:sporades_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area sporades_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:lycia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area lycia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_caria.txt
form_chrysaoric_league = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical
	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:carian # Imperator primary_culture = carian
		government_type = government_type:republic # Imperator is_republic = yes
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that CHY does not exist yet (formable_not_chrysaoric_league_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = caria_ownership_value_tooltip
			# region:asia_region = {
			# 	any_region_province = {
			# 		count >= 18
			# 		OR = { is_in_area = coastal_caria_area is_in_area = inland_caria_area }
			# 		owned_or_subject_owned = root
			# 	}
			# }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator region:asia_region was not translated
		}
	}

	name = CHRYSAORIC_NAME
	flag = CHRYSAORIC_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = CHRYSAORIC_ADJ
	tag = CHY
	color = chrysaoric_color


	form_effect = {
		# current_ruler = { add_nickname = NICKNAME_CHRYSAOREUS }
		#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator current_ruler effects
		pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = republic REFORM = theocratic_republic }
		# custom_tooltip = form_chrysaoric_league_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
		# set_capital = p:1980
		#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator set_capital was not translated
		capital ?= {
			ordered_pop = { order_by = pop_size max = 1 check_range_bounds = no save_temporary_scope_as = pa_majority_pop }
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator create_state_pop = nobles (1 pop) -> size 1 nobles with the culture and religion of the largest local pop
				limit = { exists = scope:pa_majority_pop }
				add_pop = { type = pop_type:nobles size = 1 culture = scope:pa_majority_pop.culture religion = scope:pa_majority_pop.religion }
			}
			ordered_pop = { order_by = pop_size max = 1 check_range_bounds = no save_temporary_scope_as = pa_majority_pop }
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator create_state_pop = nobles (1 pop) -> size 1 nobles with the culture and religion of the largest local pop
				limit = { exists = scope:pa_majority_pop }
				add_pop = { type = pop_type:nobles size = 1 culture = scope:pa_majority_pop.culture religion = scope:pa_majority_pop.religion }
			}
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
			# area:ionia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area ionia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:sporades_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area sporades_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:lycia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area lycia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_egypt.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Memphis (500), Bubastis (503), Pelusium (509), Mendes (534), Alexandria (516), Krokodilopolis (539), Polybiane (561), Diospolis Magna (569), Syene (591) in IR
form_egypt = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = {
				OR = {
					has_culture_group = culture_group:amazigh_group #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator north_african -> the mod's amazigh_group and libyan_group
					has_culture_group = culture_group:libyan_group
				}
			}
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:nubian_group } # Imperator meroitic_group
		}
		government_type = government_type:monarchy # Imperator is_monarchy = yes
		NOT = {
			tag = EGY
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = EGT (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		NOT = {
			tag = KSH
		}
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 20 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 20 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that EGT does not exist yet (egypt_not_existing); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		is_subject = no
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = EGYPT_NAME
	flag = EGYPTIAN_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = EGYPT_ADJECTIVE
	tag = EGT
	color = egypt_color

	locations = {
		alexandria
		aswan
		boubastis
		faiyum
		hermonthis
		memphis
		mendes
		pilousion
		teshne
	}

	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					culture = culture:egyptian # Imperator primary_culture = egyptian
				}
				NOT = {
					culture = culture:upper_egyptian # Imperator primary_culture = upper_egyptian
				}
				NOT = {
					culture = culture:faiyumic # Imperator primary_culture = middle_egyptian
				}
			}
			# set_primary_culture_cleanup_effect = { NEW_PRIMARY_CULTURE = egyptian MAKE_OLD_CULTURE_INTEGRATED = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator primary culture switch and its cleanup (culture rights, integration) not translated
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_reform = government_reform:theocratic_monarchy
				}
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = monarchy REFORM = theocratic_monarchy }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				# p:500 = { is_capital = no }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator p:500 was not translated
			}
			set_capital = location:memphis #P:A TODO Verify placement, was Memphis (500) in IR
				# p:500.state = { set_state_capital = 500 }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator province 500.state has no EU5 location
			capital ?= {
				if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
					limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
					change_location_rank = location_rank:town
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Memphis (500) in IR
		location:memphis = {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_large_effect = yes
		}
		# custom_tooltip = egypt_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
			# region:lower_egypt_region = { formable_region_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator region lower_egypt_region not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# region:upper_egypt_region = { formable_region_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator region upper_egypt_region not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { name = ach_new_kingdom_var }
		}
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_greater_aquitania .txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was area cantabria_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area autrigonia_area (geometry trigger pa_imp_in_area_autrigonia_area); area vardulia_area (geometry trigger pa_imp_in_area_vardulia_area); area vasconia_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area ilergetia_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); region aquitaine_region (province table, majority per EU5 province) in IR
form_greater_aquitania = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = culture:vasconian # Imperator primary_culture = vasconian
			culture = culture:aquitani # Imperator primary_culture = aquitani
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = AQ2 (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 20 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 20 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that AQ2 does not exist yet (greater_aquitania_not_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = GREATER_AQUITANIA_NAME
	flag = GREATER_AQUITANIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = AQUITANIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = AQ2
	color = marite_color

	areas = {
		gascony_area
		guyenne_area
		limousin_province
		navarre_area
		poitou_area
	}
	locations = {

			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}

				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_greater_maeotia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Khulor (7634), Tanais (4557), Sabiranum (7600), Gorgippia (4565), Phanagoria (4563), Pantikapaion (4549), Theodosia (4544), Taphros (4533), Limnaioi (4552), Halieuma Theou (4554) in IR
form_greater_maeotia = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = culture:maeotian # Imperator primary_culture = maeotian
		#P:A Removed: tag = GMA (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 6 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 6 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that GMA does not exist yet (formable_not_greater_maeotia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = GREATER_MAEOTIA_NAME
	flag = GMA #P:A TODO No flag: Imperator has no coat of arms GMA; the mod's random Imperator-style flag rules apply
	adjective = GREATER_MAEOTIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = GMA
	color = greater_maeotia_color

	locations = {
		bakhmut
		berdiansk
		kalay
		mapa
		or_qapi
		shyrokyne
		solkhat
		taman
		vosporo
	}

	form_effect = {
		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		capital ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					root = {
						government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
					}
				}
				pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
				pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
			}
			else = {
				province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
				pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_hellespontine_phrygia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was area aeolia_area (geometry trigger pa_imp_in_area_aeolia_area); area mysia_abrettene_area (geometry trigger pa_imp_in_area_mysia_abrettene_area); area mysia_area (geometry trigger pa_imp_in_area_mysia_area); area bithynia_area (province table, majority per EU5 province); area bithynia_superior_area (geometry trigger pa_imp_in_area_bithynia_superior_area) in IR

	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		OR = {
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:greek_group } # Imperator hellenic
			culture = {
				OR = {
					has_culture_group = culture_group:lydian_group #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator anatolian group -> the mod's lydian, lycian and luwian groups
					has_culture_group = culture_group:lycian_group
					has_culture_group = culture_group:luwian_group
				}
			}
		}
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_area_aeolia_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_mysia_abrettene_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_mysia_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_bithynia_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_bithynia_superior_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_bithynia_inferior_area = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_heracleotis_area = yes
			}
		}
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		NOR = {
			#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
			#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
			has_variable = is_pergamon
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = HPP (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
	}

	allow = {
		at_war = no
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that HPP does not exist yet (formable_not_hellespontine_phrygia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
	}

	name = HELLESPONTINE_NAME
	flag = HELLESPONTINE_PHRYGIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = HELLESPONTINE_ADJECTIVE
	tag = HPP
	color = hellespontine_phrygia_color

	locations = {
		adranos
		ayvacik
		balikesir

		bergama

	}

	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = culture:nuragic # Imperator primary_culture = nuragic
			culture = culture:corsian # Imperator primary_culture = corsian
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = SAR (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that SAR does not exist yet (formable_not_sardinia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = SARDINIA_NAME
	flag = SARDINIA_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = SARDINIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = SAR
	color = ultrasuperior_nuragic_choosen_by_sardo_himself_color

	locations = {
		ales
		alghero
		bitti
		bosa
		cagliari
		castelsardo
		gennargentu_mountains
		glarus
		isili
		lucerne
		macomer
		muravera
		oristano
		orosei
		ozieri
		posada
		sassari
		schwyz
		seddori
		sorgono
		tempiopausania
		terralba
		terranova_pausania
		thiesi
		toggenburg
		tortoli
		tratalias
		villa_di_chiesa
	}

	form_effect = {
		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				culture = culture:corsian # Imperator primary_culture = corsian
			}
		}
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_tribal_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_tribal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
		# every_country = {
		# 	limit = {
		# 		OR = {
		# 			primary_culture = nuragic
		# 			capital_scope = {
		# 				OR = { is_in_area = sardinia_meridionalis is_in_area = sardinia_septentrionalis }
		# 			}
		# 		}
		# 	}
		# 	trigger_event = {
		# 		id = me_nuragic_01_inv.1000
		# 		days = { 3 None None 5 None None }
		# 	}
		# }
		#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: nothing inside Imperator every_country could be translated
	}

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/tier_2_formables/form_scythia.txt
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Gerrhos (6145), Borysthenia (6144), Taurica (4547), Exampeaus (6119) in IR
#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Gerrhos (6145) in IR: mapped to location:mykytyn_rih, in a different EU5 region from the rest of the list; check it first
form_scythia = {
	level = 3 # Imperator tier 2
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = historical

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:western_steppe_group } # Imperator scythian_east
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_region_taurica_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_sarmatia_asiatica_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_scythia_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_area_bassetia_area = yes
			}
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = SCY (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 2 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 2 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_tier_2_formable_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that SCY does not exist yet (formable_not_scythia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = SCYTHIA_NAME
	flag = SCY #P:A mod flag
	adjective = SCYTHIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = SCY
	color = map_SCY

	locations = {
		bereznehuvate
		kezlev
		mykytyn_rih
		velyka_lepetykha
	}

	form_effect = {
		add_political_influence = 150 # Imperator monarchy_formable_government_change_effect
		if = {
			limit = {
				# p:6144 = { is_capital = no }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator p:6144 was not translated
			}
			set_capital = location:velyka_lepetykha #P:A TODO Verify placement, was Borysthenia (6144) in IR
				# p:6144.state = { set_state_capital = 6144 }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator province 6144.state has no EU5 location
			capital ?= {
				if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
					limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
					change_location_rank = location_rank:town
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Borysthenia (6144) in IR
		location:velyka_lepetykha = {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		# custom_tooltip = form_lesser_scythia_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
			# area:borysthenia_minor_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area borysthenia_minor_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
			# area:borysthenia_area = { formable_area_province_effect = yes }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: claims on every province of the Imperator area borysthenia_area not owned yet (EU5 has no claims)
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = tribe REFORM = tribal_federation }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:republic # Imperator is_republic = yes
			}
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

	}

		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = AKSUM_NAME
	flag = AX2 #P:A TODO No flag: Imperator has no coat of arms AX2; the mod's random Imperator-style flag rules apply
	adjective = AKSUM_ADJECTIVE
	tag = AX2
	# color #P:A TODO Imperator gave no map colour; the country keeps its old map colour

		adigrat
		axum
		dallol
	}

		pa_imp_tribal_formable_government_change_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { name = aksum_formed }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					capital ?= {
						this = location:axum # Imperator Aksum (7506)
					}
				}
			}
			set_capital = location:axum #P:A TODO Verify placement, was Aksum (7506) in IR
				# p:7506.state = { set_state_capital = 7506 }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator province 7506.state has no EU5 location
			capital ?= {
				if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
					limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
					change_location_rank = location_rank:town
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A TODO Verify placement, was Aksum (7506) in IR
		location:axum = {
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		# custom_tooltip = form_aksum_claims_tooltip #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/endgame_tags/form_bharatavarsha.txt
form_bharatavarsha = {
	level = 4 # Imperator endgame tags
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = plausible
	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:indo_aryan_group } # Imperator aryan
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:pracyan_group } # Imperator pracyan
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:dravidian_group } #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator indian -> dravidian_group (maps/tables.json)
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = BHA (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 50 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 50 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that BHA does not exist yet (bharatavarsha_not_existing); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		is_subject = no
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			any_owned_location = {
				# is_in_india_trigger = yes
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator is_in_india_trigger was not translated
				count >= 680
			}
		}
		trigger_else = {
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_pracya_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = pracya_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_madhyadesa_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = madhyadesa_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_vindhyaprstha_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = vindhyaprstha_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_dravida_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = dravida_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_aparanta_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = aparanta_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_avanti_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = avanti_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_maru_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = maru_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_gandhara_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = gandhara_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_karnata_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = karnata_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
		}
	}

	name = BHARATVARSHA_NAME
	flag = BHARAT_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = BHARATVARSHA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = BHA
	color = bharat_green_color


	form_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					# government = imperium
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government imperium
					has_reform = government_reform:imperial_cult
					# heritage = yaudheya_heritage
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator heritage was not translated
				}
			}
			# republic_to_monarchy_law_variable_effect = yes
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator republic_to_monarchy_law_variable_effect was not translated
			# change_government = imperium
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government imperium
			# republic_to_monarchy_law_change_effect = yes
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator republic_to_monarchy_law_change_effect was not translated
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				# heritage = yaudheya_heritage
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator heritage was not translated
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = monarchy REFORM = dictatorship }
		}
		pa_imp_add_5_free_province_investments = yes
		set_variable = { name = bharat_formed days = 9125 }
	}
}

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					culture = culture:scandian # Imperator primary_culture = scandian
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					# tag = ENG
