				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					# tag = FRA
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator tag FRA has no matching mod country
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = francia_red_map_color
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = francia_green_map_color
					}
					# region:lower_egypt_region = {
					# 	every_region_province = { add_claim = root }
					# }
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator region:lower_egypt_region was not translated
					# region:upper_egypt_region = {
					# 	every_region_province = { add_claim = root }
					# }
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator region:upper_egypt_region was not translated
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = francia_red_flag
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = francia_green_flag
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					culture = culture:indo_germanic # Imperator primary_culture = indo_germanic
				}
				# trigger_event = { id = flavor_germanics.900 }
				#P:A TODO Associated event was not imported: flavor_germanics.900
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = francia_green_map_color
			}
			# current_ruler = { add_nickname = NICKNAME_THE_GREEN_BLIGHT }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator current_ruler effects
			# custom_tooltip = green_germania_claims_tt #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: announces the claims below, which EU5 cannot give
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				culture = culture:indo_germanic # Imperator primary_culture = indo_germanic
			}
			# current_ruler = { add_nickname = NICKNAME_THE_RECLAIMER }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator current_ruler effects
			custom_tooltip = indo_germanic_germania_event_tt
		}
		capital ?= {
			pa_imp_capital_formable_large_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_large_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_monarchy_republic_into_imperium_formable_effect = yes
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = formable_country_huge_modifier
			days = 7600
		}
		pa_imp_add_5_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

form_iberia = {
	level = 4 # Imperator endgame tags
	required_locations_fraction = 0.8 #P:A TODO Approximate: EU5 provinces stand in for Imperator areas/regions
	rule = plausible

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:iberia_group } # Imperator iberia
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:celtiberian_group } # Imperator celt_iberia
		}
		NOT = {
			culture = culture:aquitani # Imperator primary_culture = aquitani
		}
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_region_baetica_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_tarraconensis_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_contestania_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_gallaecia_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_lusitania_region = yes

		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 100 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 100 (territories); EU5 owned locations

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that HBR does not exist yet (formable_not_iberia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify

			government_type = government_type:monarchy # Imperator is_monarchy = yes

	adjective = IBERIA_ADJECTIVE
	tag = HBR

#P:A Imported from Invictus: decisions/endgame_tags/form_indo_germanic_kingdom.txt
form_indger = {
	level = 4 # Imperator endgame tags
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = plausible
	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:germanic_group } # Imperator germanic
		#P:A Removed: tag = IGK (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_region_pracya_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_madhyadesa_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_vindhyaprstha_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_dravida_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_aparanta_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_karnata_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_avanti_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_maru_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_gandhara_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_burma_region = yes
			}
		}
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations <= 12 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities <= 12 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that IGK does not exist yet (indger_not_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_pracya_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = pracya_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_madhyadesa_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = madhyadesa_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_vindhyaprstha_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = vindhyaprstha_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_dravida_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = dravida_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_aparanta_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = aparanta_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_karnata_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = karnata_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_avanti_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = avanti_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_maru_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = maru_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
			pa_imp_owns_ir_region_gandhara_region = yes #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator owns_or_subject_owns_region = gandhara_region; EU5 provinces standing in for it
		}
	}

	name = INDGER_NAME
	flag = INDGER_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = INDGER_ADJECTIVE
	tag = IGK
	color = dark_rust


	form_effect = {
		hidden_effect = {
			root = {
				# save_scope_as = indo_germanic_kingdom
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator save_scope_as was not translated
				# current_ruler = { save_scope_as = indo_germanic_kingdom_ruler }
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator current_ruler effects
			}
			# every_country = {
			# 	limit = {
			# 		NOT = { this = root }
			# 	}
			# 	trigger_event = { id = me_indo_germanic_kingdom.900 }
			# }
			#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: nothing inside Imperator every_country could be translated
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			province ?= { pa_imp_capital_formable_medium_effect = yes } # province-scope effect of the event import
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_normal_effect = yes
		}
		pa_imp_add_3_free_province_investments = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = monarchy REFORM = despotic_monarchy }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = igk_mission_access_tt
	}
}

form_indo_parthia = {
	level = 4 # Imperator endgame tags
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = plausible
	potential_requires_own = no #P:A TODO Imperator expressed the requirement in allow only (alternatives or counts); no EU5 geography list

	potential = {
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		#P:A Removed: tag = INP (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		culture = culture:parthian # Imperator primary_culture = parthian
		capital ?= {
			OR = {
				pa_imp_in_region_pracya_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_madhyadesa_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_vindhyaprstha_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_dravida_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_aparanta_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_karnata_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_avanti_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_maru_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_gandhara_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_burma_region = yes
				pa_imp_in_region_ariana_region = yes
			}
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that INP does not exist yet (formable_not_indo_parthia_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		trigger_if = {
			limit = {
				#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = indoparthia_decision.tt1
				NOT = {
					any_owned_location = {
						pa_imp_in_region_parthia_region = yes
					}
					any_owned_location = {
						pa_imp_in_region_media_region = yes
					}
					any_owned_location = {
						pa_imp_in_region_persis_region = yes
					}
					any_owned_location = {
						pa_imp_in_region_mesopotamia_region = yes
					}
					any_owned_location = {
						pa_imp_in_region_assyria_region = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = indoparthia_decision.tt2
			owns = location:rohri # Imperator Roruka (4352)
			owns = location:kandiaro # Imperator Brahmanaka (4353)
			owns = location:sehwan # Imperator Sindhumana (4355)
			owns = location:uch # Imperator Deogarh (4371)
			owns = location:kot_addu # Imperator Mulasthanapura (4350)
			owns = location:multan # Imperator Mallia (4349)
			owns = location:tulamba # Imperator Anatachara (4382)
			owns = location:kamalia # Imperator Kot Kamalia (9280)
			owns = location:sialkot # Imperator Sakala (4340)
			owns = location:virkgarh # Imperator Madra (4346)

	}

				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}

				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
			pa_imp_change_ir_government_effect = { TYPE = tribe REFORM = tribal_federation }
			pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
		}
		capital ?= {
			if = { #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator establish_city_effect (settlement -> city); EU5 rural settlement -> town
				limit = { location_rank = location_rank:rural_settlement }
				change_location_rank = location_rank:town
			}
			pa_imp_capital_formable_large_effect = yes
			pa_imp_formable_capital_modifier_large_effect = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:germanic_group } # Imperator germanic
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						culture = {
							OR = {
								has_culture_group = culture_group:scythia_group #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator scythia group -> the mod's scythia_group and saka_group (maps/tables.json picks saka_group)
								has_culture_group = culture_group:saka_group
							}
						}
						culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:western_steppe_group } # Imperator scythian_east
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					culture = culture:pannonian # Imperator primary_culture = pannonian
				}
			}
		}
		pa_imp_add_5_free_province_investments = yes
	}
}

form_persia = {
	level = 4 # Imperator endgame tags
	required_locations_fraction = 1

	potential = {

			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:persian_group } # Imperator persia
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:caucasian_group } # Imperator caucasian
			culture = {
				OR = {
					has_culture_group = culture_group:lydian_group #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator anatolian group -> the mod's lydian, lycian and luwian groups
					has_culture_group = culture_group:lycian_group
					has_culture_group = culture_group:luwian_group
				}
			}
		}
		#P:A Removed: tag = PER (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 40 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 40 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
		#P:A Removed: is_endgame_tag_trigger = yes (Imperator formable tiers; EU5 compares formable levels itself)
	}

		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that PER does not exist yet ("persia_not_exists"); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify

				this = culture:pasargadi # Imperator is_culture = pasargadi
			}
		}
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	flag = ACHAEMENID_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = ACHAEMENID_ADJECTIVE

reunite_alexanders_empire = {
	level = 4 # Imperator endgame tags
	required_locations_fraction = 1
	rule = plausible

	potential = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = formed_argead_empire
		}
		num_locations >= 1 # Imperator num_of_cities
		OR = {
			culture = { has_culture_group = culture_group:greek_group } # Imperator hellenic
			has_variable = pentapotamia_hellenic_empire
		}
		government_type = government_type:monarchy # Imperator is_monarchy = yes
		#P:A Removed: tag = ALX (the formable's own tag; EU5 formables do not check it)
		OR = {
			is_ai = no
			num_locations >= 40 #P:A TODO Approximate: Imperator num_of_cities >= 40 (territories); EU5 owned locations
		}
	}

	allow = {
		#P:A Removed: Imperator checked that ALX does not exist yet (formable_not_argeads_exists); EU5 formables cannot form an existing tag #P:A TODO Verify
		government_type = government_type:monarchy # Imperator is_monarchy = yes
		pa_imp_can_form_nation_trigger = yes
	}

	name = ALX_NAME
	flag = ARGEAD_FLAG #P:A existing coat of arms
	adjective = ALX_ADJECTIVE
	tag = ALX
	color = argead_blue

	locations = {
		alexandria
		antioch
		athens
		babylon
		corinth
		gallipoli
		gaza
		hamadan
		istakhr
		laribandar
		manisa
		memphis
		pella
		pindi_gheb
		seleukia
		sidon
		siwa
	}

	form_effect = {
		set_variable = formed_argead_empire
		if = {
			limit = {
				government_type = government_type:tribe # Imperator is_tribal = yes
			}
			add_country_modifier = {
				modifier = civilized_tribe
				days = 5475
			}
		}
		add_country_modifier = {
			modifier = argead_empire_modifier
			days = -1
		}
		pa_imp_add_5_free_province_investments = yes
		# trigger_event = { id = diadochi_flavor.7 }
		#P:A TODO Associated event was not imported: diadochi_flavor.7
		# trigger_event = { id = diadochi_flavor.200 }
		#P:A TODO Associated event was not imported: diadochi_flavor.200
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						# government = imperium
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government imperium
						has_reform = government_reform:imperial_cult
						# government = spartan_monarchy
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_monarchy
						# government = spartan_imperium
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_imperium
						# government = spartan_imperial_cult
						#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_imperial_cult
					}
				}
				# republic_to_monarchy_law_variable_effect = yes
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator republic_to_monarchy_law_variable_effect was not translated
				# change_government = imperium
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government imperium
				# republic_to_monarchy_law_change_effect = yes
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: Imperator republic_to_monarchy_law_change_effect was not translated
				pa_imp_switch_government_type_event_clearup_effect = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					# government = spartan_imperial_cult
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_imperial_cult
					# government = spartan_imperium
					#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_imperium
					always = no #P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_monarchy
					# government = spartan_monarchy
				}
				# change_government = spartan_imperium
				#P:A TODO No EU5 equivalent: the mod has no reform for the Imperator government spartan_imperium
